TL;DR: 本文提出Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy（CoPES），将完整参数空间分解为低维子空间协同搜索，在资源受限（少GPU）场景下显著提高进化策略后训练工具使用LLM代理的效率，在GPU时预算下恢复GRPO 92%的验证准确率增益（标准ES仅67%），内存需求不足GRPO的八分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的LLM代理产生长多轮轨迹，基于梯度的后训练内存开销大；进化策略（ES）虽无需反向传播、内存高效，但在仅少量GPU的资源受限环境下，其高GPU时需求导致训练时间过长，亟需提升优化效率。

Method: CoPES采用协作协同进化方法，将完整参数空间分解为低维子空间，并在这些子空间上协同搜索以提高优化效率。作者对Qwen3.5-4B工具使用代理进行数学任务后训练，并在五个不同难度的基准上评估。

Result: 在全参数GRPO最优验证检查点的GPU时预算下，CoPES恢复了GRPO验证准确率增益的92%（标准ES仅67%）；理论GPU内存需求不足全参数GRPO的八分之一；在五个基准的所有pass@k指标上一致优于标准ES和基于LoRA的GRPO；额外实验在问答任务上也展现优势。

Conclusion: CoPES在资源受限条件下为智能体LLM后训练提供了内存需求与训练时间之间更好的权衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents produce long, multi-turn trajectories, making gradient-based post-training memory-intensive. Evolution strategies (ES) enable memory-efficient full-parameter post-training without backpropagation and can eventually match the performance of gradient-based reinforcement learning (RL). However, resource-constrained settings typically offer only a few GPUs, so the high GPU-hour requirements of ES translate into prohibitively long training times. To address this, we introduce Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES), a cooperative coevolutionary method that decomposes the full parameter space into lower-dimensional subspaces and searches over them cooperatively to improve optimization efficiency. We post-train a Qwen3.5-4B tool-using agent for the math task and evaluate it on five benchmarks of varying difficulty. Under the GPU-hour budget of full-parameter GRPO's best validation checkpoint, CoPES recovers 92% of GRPO's validation-accuracy gain, versus 67% for standard ES, while its theoretical GPU memory requirement is less than one-eighth that of full-parameter GRPO. It consistently outperforms standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO on all evaluated pass@k metrics across the five benchmarks. Additional experiments further show the advantage of CoPES on the question-answering task. These results demonstrate an improved trade-off between memory requirements and training time for agentic LLM post-training under resource constraints. The code is open-sourced in https://github.com/MetaronWang/CoPES

</details>


### [218] [MonitrLLM: A Community-Centered Evaluation Infrastructure for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02409)
*Victor Ojewale,Ro Encarnación,Suresh Venkatasubramanian,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍MonitrLLM——一个开源的基础设施，用于社区中心化的LLM评估，将完整对话记录与用户报告的任务意图和结果评估联系起来。通过26名大学生使用ChatGPT的两周试点实验（206份评估报告），发现用户虽然报告高平均满意度（4.19/5），但目标任务失败率高达23.1%，且多轮对话的失败率是单轮对话的2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估存在关键空白：基准测试在受控任务上评估能力，大规模语料库捕捉自然使用但缺乏用户反馈，界面内反馈机制记录满意度但缺乏任务目的。缺乏将交互轨迹与用户自定义结果联系起来的基础设施。

Method: 设计并构建MonitrLLM开源基础设施，将完整对话记录、用户报告的任务意图和结果评估三者作为主要评估信号。开展了为期两周的可行性试点，招募26名大学生使用ChatGPT，收集206份包含完整对话记录的评估报告。

Result: 试点显示用户报告的高满意度（4.19/5）与23.1%的高任务失败率并存；多轮对话失败率是单轮对话的2.5倍，表明长对话可能反映任务困难而非用户参与度高。

Conclusion: 将直接用户反馈与观测数据相结合对稳健的LLM评估具有重要价值，并展望了实现这一目标的基础设施可能性。

Abstract: Benchmark suites assess model capability on controlled tasks; large-scale conversation corpora capture naturalistic use without user feedback; and in-interface feedback mechanisms record satisfaction without task purpose. Together, they leave a critical gap in LLM evaluation: no existing infrastructure routinely links interaction trajectories to user-defined outcomes. We introduce MonitrLLM, open-source infrastructure for community-centered LLM evaluations that links full conversation transcripts to user-reported task intent and outcome assessments, treating all three as primary evaluative signals rather than optional metadata.
  To demonstrate the value of this approach, we conducted a two-week feasibility pilot with 26 college students using ChatGPT, collecting 206 evaluation reports with full conversation transcripts. The findings from our pilot demonstrate the value of connecting conversation trajectories with user-reported outcomes. For instance, despite reporting high average satisfaction (4.19/5) with their LLM interactions, participants also experience a substantial 23.1% failure rate on their goal tasks. We also find that multi-turn conversations are reported as failing at 2.5 times the rate of single-turn exchanges, a pattern that reframes extended interaction as a signal of difficulty rather than engagement. We conclude by discussing the value of incorporating direct user feedback with observational data for robust LLM evaluations, and the possibilities for infrastructure that enables this goal.

</details>


### [219] [xPress: Parallel Refinement for Diffusion Drafters in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02438)
*Zheng Wang,Davis Wertheimer,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Mudhakar Srivatsa,Raghu K. Ganti,Minjia Zhang,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出xPress，一种轻量级因果精修器，用于修复扩散式草稿生成器（如dFlash）中因逐位置独立采样而缺失的因果依赖，从而提升投机解码的接受长度和端到端吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dFlash等扩散式草稿模型通过单次前向传播生成整块草稿token，大幅降低了投机解码中多次token起草的开销。然而，其离散去噪的最终步骤是对每个位置独立采样token，导致生成的是逐位置边际分布的集合而非联合分布，草稿token之间缺乏因果依赖。这种独立采样的边际分布容易产生"单个token看似合理但整体联合概率低下"的序列，导致目标模型在逐token条件验证时过早拒绝，限制了接受长度。

Method: xPress通过并行精修的方式恢复扩散草稿器中缺失的因果性。它是一个轻量级因果精修器，在单次前向传播中同时协调整个扩散块，通过并行精修来恢复并传播草稿中的因果依赖关系，避免了逐token循环的开销。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，覆盖七项数学、代码和聊天基准测试，xPress相比原始dFlash扩散草稿器，平均将接受长度提升约30%（最高达+56%），端到端解码吞吐量平均提升约1.3倍（最高达1.7倍）。

Conclusion: xPress有效解决了扩散式草稿生成器中因逐位置独立采样导致的因果缺失问题，在保持单次前向传播高效性的同时，显著提升了投机解码的接受长度和整体吞吐性能。

Abstract: Block-diffusion drafters like dFlash generate an entire block of draft tokens in a single forward pass, drastically reducing the overhead of multiple-token drafting in speculative decoding. The crucial final step of the single-pass discrete denoising process involves using the logit distribution at each position to sample conditionally independent tokens. The resulting draft is thus a set of per-position marginals, rather than a joint distribution: no draft token is guaranteed to depend on its predecessors. Such independently sampled marginals tend to produce sequences with tokens that are individually likely, but jointly improbable under the target model's distribution, which verifies each token conditionally. This can cause early rejection and limits acceptance length. To address this, we propose xPress as a means to restore the missing causality in diffusion drafters. xPress is a lightweight causal refiner that reconciles the whole diffusion block at once through parallel refinement, restoring and propagating causal dependencies across the draft without a token-by-token loop. On Qwen3-8B, across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, xPress raises acceptance length by about 30% on average (up to +56%) and its end-to-end decoding throughput by about 1.3 on average (up to 1.7) compared to the original dFlash diffusion drafter.

</details>


### [220] [Real-Time Detection and Repair of LLM Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02464)
*Sunny Dubey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究仅从步骤遥测数据检测LLM智能体失败的方法，提出回声状态网络集成加CUSUM告警的一类监控器，辅以确定性验证层，能以极低成本检测失败并闭环修复，将任务成功率从52%提升至73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在运行中经常失败（死循环、工具错误级联、偏离目标、伪造结果等），而标准的修复方案——用第二个LLM逐步骤评判——成本比智能体本身还高。作者希望探索仅从可观测的步骤遥测数据中能实现多少失败检测，使用微秒级监控器且仅需健康运行数据进行训练。

Method: 提出两层检测框架：(1) 一类回声状态网络集成（one-class echo-state-network ensemble）配合CUSUM告警，仅从步骤遥测数据以微秒级开销检测异常；(2) 确定性验证层，重算运行的声称总数与实际工具结果对比，并确认所有必要调用是否完成。检测后对标记的运行进行回滚并实时重跑实现修复。

Result: 在2823个智能体运行片段（跨3个框架、3个本地模型和1个商业API）上，监控器在5%误报预算下检测出0.71的失败（AUROC 0.872）；确定性验证以0个假阳性捕获60%失败（含覆盖率检查时达96%）；回滚重跑恢复45%失败（对照16%），任务成功率从52%提升至73%，每个步骤仅约200微秒开销。

Conclusion: 仅基于步骤遥测的轻量级监控加确定性验证即可实现高效的LLM智能体失败检测与修复，成本比判官LLM低三个数量级。但监控器存在部署时健康基线需求和残余误报率，确定性验证层则无这两项负担，二者互补。

Abstract: LLM agents fail mid-episode -- they loop, cascade tool errors, drift off goal, fabricate results, or silently absorb corrupted content -- and the standard remedy, judging every step with a second LLM, costs more than the agent itself. We ask how much detection is achievable from observable step telemetry alone, using monitors costing microseconds per step and trained only on healthy runs.
  On 2,823 committed agent episodes across three frameworks, three local models (qwen2.5 7b/3b, llama3.1 8b) and a commercial API (gemini-2.5-flash), a one-class echo-state-network ensemble with CUSUM alarms detects 0.71 of failures at a 5% false-alarm budget (AUROC 0.872). Its advantage over a memoryless baseline is a monotone function of post-onset horizon (+0.09 at <=3 steps, +0.40 at >=9), predicting its own failure region out of sample on AFTraj-2K. Ranking transfers with no retraining to two corpora from other groups (AFTraj-2K 0.745, ATBench 0.779).
  Monitors carry two burdens: a per-deployment healthy null (they do not transfer -- AUROC 0.527 cold against 0.885 recalibrated) and a residual false-alarm rate. We add a layer carrying neither: deterministic verification, which recomputes a run's stated total from the tool results it actually received and confirms every required call was made. Head-to-head it catches 60% of failures (96% with the coverage check) at 0 of 63 false positives against the monitor's 54% at 17%, transfers unchanged to llama3.1:8b (110 of 110 at 0 of 10), and trips on 0 of 1825 healthy episodes.
  Detection is then closed into repair: each flagged run is rolled back and re-run live, recovering 45% of failures against a 16% resampling control (p=0.0005) and lifting task success from 52% to 73% for about one extra model call per run. The system runs at ~200 microseconds per step, three orders of magnitude below a judge call. Code, traces and results are released.

</details>


### [221] [Agentic Commerce World: An Auditable and Verifiable Environment for Vibe Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02441)
*Shicheng Fan,Mingdai Yang,Duohao Wang,Canyu Chen,Yongfeng Zhang,Hua Wei,Manling Li,Julian McAuley,Kun Zhang,Philip S. Yu,Kejing Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ACWorld（Agentic Commerce World），一个用于评估自然语言驱动的买家和商家智能体在共享市场中交互的环境，包含Vibe Commerce Protocol协议和200+60个任务的基准测试，实验表明十个模型在两个轨道的平均得分范围分别为65.9%-85.6%和56.1%-91.4%，并强调过程级证据对智能体评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借鉴vibe coding的成功经验，作者提出vibe commerce的概念，即用户用自然语言表达买卖目标并委托给AI智能体执行。然而商务场景的特殊性在于需要独立的买家和商家智能体在共享市场中交互，同时保护各自的私有目标和不同权限，这需要一个专门的环境来评估这类多智能体交互系统。

Method: 作者构建了ACWorld环境，通过Vibe Commerce Protocol（VCP）协议在更新共享交易状态前验证智能体动作并记录交互过程，使行为可审计、评估可复现。基准测试包含两个轨道：200个任务的能力覆盖轨道和60个任务的大目录轨道（搜索785,022个可交易列表）。

Result: 在十个模型上，能力覆盖轨道平均得分范围为65.9%至85.6%，大目录轨道为56.1%至91.4%。分析发现：仅依赖最终状态可能遗漏评估错误；不完整的轨迹仍保留有用的过程信号；大目录任务暴露了各阶段瓶颈。

Conclusion: 过程级证据对评估商务智能体至关重要，ACWorld为vibe commerce场景下的多智能体评估提供了可复现、可审计的标准化环境，揭示了大目录场景下智能体性能的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: In vibe coding, people describe software in natural language and delegate implementation to AI agents. By analogy, vibe commerce allows people to express buying or selling goals in natural language and delegate the corresponding tasks to agents. Commerce, however, requires independently controlled Buyer and Merchant agents to interact in a shared market while preserving their private objectives and distinct authority. We introduce Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld), an environment for evaluating such agents across ongoing transactions. Through its Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP), ACWorld validates agent actions before updating shared transaction state and records the resulting interactions, making agent behavior auditable and evaluation reproducible. The ACWorld Benchmark contains a 200-task capability-coverage track and a 60-task large-catalog track that searches 785,022 transactable listings. Across ten models, mean scores range from 65.9% to 85.6% and from 56.1% to 91.4%, respectively. Our analysis shows that process-level evidence is necessary: final state alone can miss evaluated errors, incomplete trajectories still retain useful process signals, and large-catalog tasks expose bottlenecks across stages.

</details>


### [222] [Optimizing Minimax Regret in Uncertain MDPs with Small Sets of Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02509)
*Sterre Lutz,Daniël Vos,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Anna Lukina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了不确定马尔可夫决策过程（UMDP）中的k-自适应策略合成问题，即在执行前才确定环境模型的情况下，事先准备k个策略并从中选择最合适的一个，以最小化最坏情况下的遗憾值。作者证明了该问题是NP难的，并提出了KAPS算法（精确的嵌套分支定界算法），实验表明从1个策略增加到2个策略时遗憾值的降低最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的序贯决策往往面临环境模型的不确定性。在UMDP中，单一策略可能牺牲性能，而为每个可能的MDP都准备单独优化的策略又会违反操作、监管或可解释性方面的约束（策略数量受限）。作者希望找到一种在策略数量有限的情况下，通过预先准备一组策略来平衡性能与约束的方法。

Method: 作者提出了k-自适应策略合成问题，目标是在最小最大遗憾（minimax-regret）目标下优化一组k个策略。核心方法KAPS是一个精确的嵌套分支定界算法，包含问题特定的界限和启发式方法。该算法联合优化两个子问题：哪些MDP共享同一个策略，以及这些策略本身的最优设计。

Result: 实验在多种UMDP基准上进行，发现从1个策略增加到2个策略时遗憾值的降低最为显著。在单策略设置下，KAPS在解的质量上与现有方法相当，并且能够更频繁地证明最优性。

Conclusion: 本文为UMDP中有限策略集的最优合成提供了理论保障（NP-hard性证明）和精确求解算法KAPS，填补了该领域精确方法的空白。实验结果揭示了策略数量与遗憾值之间的权衡关系，为实际应用中选择适当的策略数量提供了指导。

Abstract: Sequential decision-making in real-world applications often involves uncertainty about the environment's model. Uncertain Markov decision processes (UMDPs) represent the possible environments as a set of MDPs with shared states and actions but potentially different transition probabilities and rewards. Optimizing a single policy across all possible MDPs may sacrifice performance, while preparing an individually optimized policy for every MDP may violate operational, regulatory, or interpretability constraints on the number of policies that can be prepared and deployed. We consider settings in which model uncertainty is resolved shortly before execution, allowing the most suitable policy to be selected from a limited set prepared in advance. We introduce $k$-adaptable policy synthesis, which optimizes such a set of $k$ policies under a minimax-regret objective. We prove that the problem is NP-hard and develop KAPS, an exact nested branch-and-bound algorithm with problem-specific bounds and heuristics. KAPS jointly optimizes which MDPs share a policy and the policies themselves. Experiments across various UMDP benchmarks show that the largest reduction in regret consistently occurs when increasing from one to two policies. In the single-policy setting, KAPS is competitive with existing methods in solution quality and proves optimality substantially more often.

</details>


### [223] [Right Answer, Wrong Method: Shortcut Hacking Misleads the Evaluation of LLM Reasoning on Frontier Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02442)
*Xuan Ren,Weiqi Zhai,Tianle Pu,Yihua Zhu,Yihua Zhu,Hu Wei,Bing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在科学推理基准测试中存在"Solution Hacking"（解黑客）现象，即模型通过数值搜索、枚举、猜测或先验证答案再推导等捷径获得正确答案，而非真正执行任务目标所要求的推理过程。随着基准难度增加，解黑客现象从常见问题的2.2%飙升至奥林匹克级别的28.3%和HLE的37.4%，前沿模型中8.2%-44.1%的正确答案被识别为黑客解。作者还开发了反黑客策略（自动评判器和测试时指令），发现抑制捷径行为会显著降低报告准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学推理基准测试仅以最终答案准确率评估大语言模型，但正确答案并不一定反映模型真正掌握了问题所针对的推理能力，这可能导致对前沿模型科学推理能力的过度高估。

Method: 1）系统性识别"Solution Hacking"这一失败模式，将其定义为通过无效捷径（数值搜索、枚举、猜测、先验证答案再推导）达到正确答案；2）跨难度等级、科学领域和前沿模型进行分析；3）开发专家启发的反黑客策略，包括自动评判器和测试时指令。

Result: 解黑客比例随难度增加而急剧上升：从常见问题的2.2%到奥林匹克级问题的28.3%，再到HLE的37.4%。前沿模型中被记为正确的答案中有8.2%-44.1%被识别为黑客解。应用反黑客策略后，报告准确率显著下降，但对真实正确的非黑客推理准确率影响较小。

Conclusion: 仅基于答案的评估会高估前沿大语言模型的科学推理能力，需要新的评估方法或指标来更准确地衡量模型真正的推理能力。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate large language models (LLMs) using final-answer accuracy. However, a correct answer does not necessarily demonstrate the reasoning capability targeted by the problem. We identify Solution Hacking, a failure mode in which an LLM reaches the correct answer through invalid shortcuts, such as numerical search, enumeration, guessing, or answer-first verification, without providing a valid task-targeted derivation. We systematically analyze this phenomenon across difficulty levels, scientific domains, and frontier models. Solution hacking increases sharply with benchmark difficulty, from 2.2\% on common problems to 28.3\% on Olympiad-level problems and 37.4\% on HLE. Moreover, 8.2\%-44.1\% of answers credited as correct across frontier models are identified as hacked solutions. We further develop expert-inspired anti-hacking strategies, including an automatic judge and a test-time instruction. The results show that suppressing shortcut behavior substantially reduces reported accuracy while having a smaller effect on correct and non-hacked accuracy. These findings reveal that answer-only evaluation can overestimate the scientific reasoning capabilities of frontier LLMs.

</details>


### [224] [ParEvalLayer: When Partial LLM-Agent Evaluations Support a Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02444)
*Wei-Jung Huang,Bonan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParEvalLayer，一种决策层框架，用于在LLM-agent评估部分完成时判断是否可提前得出与完整评估一致的结论。通过重放公开基准数据，发现部分基准仅需15%-25%的任务结果即可得出与完整评估相同的结论，而其他基准需要更多数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent评估常常在基准测试完全完成之前就产生任务结果，但部分分数无法显示已观察任务是否支持与完整评估相同的结论。早期任务可能遗漏基准的重要部分，先运行较便宜的任务会扭曲样本，且仅对简单样本做决策的规则可能看起来准确但留下大量未解决比较。因此需要一种方法来判断部分运行结果是否足以达成可靠结论。

Method: 提出ParEvalLayer决策层，读取两个agent系统的成对结果以及预先选择的比较策略。对于每次部分运行，它记录测试系统是否达到所需优势量、未达到该优势量、需要更多证据或应弃权。作者通过重放已完成的公共基准数据，模拟每次评估提前停止的情况，在每个时间点仅使用已观察结果应用策略，并检查判断是否与完整数据一致。

Result: 使用主要比较规则时，三个公共基准在仅观察15%至25%的任务结果后即达到与完整评估相同的决策，而其他基准需要更多任务结果才能得出相同结论。

Conclusion: 仅报告部分分数是不够的，评估报告还应说明所使用的决策规则以及尚未做出决策的比较数量，以提供更可靠和透明的评估结论。

Abstract: LLM-agent evaluations often produce task outcomes long before the full benchmark run is complete. A partial score is tempting to report, but it does not show whether the observed tasks support the same conclusion as the completed evaluation. Early tasks can omit important parts of a benchmark, running cheaper tasks first can distort the observed sample, and a rule that decides only easy pairs can appear accurate while leaving many comparisons unresolved. We introduce ParEvalLayer, a decision layer that reads paired outcomes for two agent systems and a comparison policy chosen in advance. For each partial run, it records whether the tested agent system is better by the required amount, is not better by that amount, needs more evidence, or should abstain. We evaluate ParEvalLayer by replaying completed public benchmark data as if each evaluation had stopped earlier. At each point, ParEvalLayer applies the policy using only the outcomes observed so far; if it reaches one of the two comparison judgments, we check whether that judgment matches the completed data for the same system pair. With the main comparison rule, three of the public benchmarks reach the same decision as the completed evaluation after observing only 15% to 25% of task outcomes. Other benchmarks require more task outcomes. This variation shows why a partial score alone is not enough: reports should also state the decision rule and how many comparisons remain without a decision.

</details>


### [225] [Infinite Trace Objectives with Finite Trace Techniques: Translating LTL to LTLf+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02454)
*Christoph Weinhuber,Maximilian Prokop,Giuseppe De Giacomo,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个从LTL到LTLf+的翻译方法，通过对LTL公式进行规范化处理并将其分解为LTLf+的语法片段，使得为LTLf+开发的高效推理技术可以迁移到LTL问题上，且翻译过程无渐近成本增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL在AI中被广泛使用，但传统求解LTL问题需要将其转换为无限词上的非确定性自动机再进行确定化，这在理论和实践上都很困难。LTLf+保留了LTL的表达能力，却能在有限词上的有限自动机上进行推理，具有规范最小表示和高效确定化过程。因此，将LTL翻译为LTLf+可以绕过复杂的确定化难题。

Method: 首先将LTL公式规范化为Manna-Pnueli层次结构中的语法反应性片段，以构建LTLf+的通用基于片段的形状；然后为该片段的每个独立组件提供线性翻译。

Result: 成功实现了首个从LTL到LTLf+的翻译，且该翻译无渐近成本增加——从LTL到LTLf+再到自动机的完整流水线仍保持双指数复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过将LTL转化为LTLf+，使得为LTLf+开发的大量推理技术可以应用于众多以LTL表述的AI问题，为AI领域中的时序推理提供了更高效的路径。

Abstract: Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) is one of the most widely adopted languages for specifying temporal extended objectives in AI, with applications ranging from reactive synthesis to stochastic planning in Markov decision processes and reinforcement learning. Traditionally, solving any of these problems requires translating the LTL specification to a nondeterministic automata on infinite words and then determinizing it, a step that is notoriously difficult in theory and in practice. Recent work has introduced LTLf+, which lifts the finite-trace logic LTLf to infinite traces. LTLf+ has the same expressive power as LTL, yet it retains most of the crucial advantages of its base logic LTLf. Most reasoning in LTLf+ rests on finite automata on finite words, for which we have not only a canonical minimal representation but also an efficient determinization procedure. In this work we present the first translation from LTL to LTLf+. We first normalize an LTL formula into the syntactic reactivity fragment of the Manna-Pnueli hierarchy, to create the general fragment-based shape of LTLf+. We then present linear translations for each individual component of that fragment. As a consequence of this translation, the expanding body of techniques developed for LTLf+ now becomes available to many AI problems currently formulated in LTL. We further show that this comes at no asymptotic cost, as the pipeline from LTL to automaton via LTLf+ remains doubly exponential.

</details>


### [226] [Long-term Measurements: Towards a Longitudinal Understanding of Human-AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02491)
*Nicole Mitchell,Dhruv Agarwal,Maty Bohacek,Remi Denton,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NLP领域需要从静态、短期的文本评估转向对人与模型交互的长期行为变化的历时性测量，借鉴社会科学中的纵向数据测量方法，以识别和缓解语言模型对用户认知、发展和社会情感层面的长期风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型因其"类人性"和快速融入用户日常生活，可能带来认知、发展和社会情感层面的纵向风险，这些风险在短期交互中难以显现，但会对用户产生持久的长期影响。现有的静态、短期评估方式无法捕捉这些风险。

Method: 借鉴社会科学中用于理解纵向数据新兴现象的测量方法，将NLP中的计算方法与这些测量手段相结合，以建模用户行为随模型交互的变化，实现在线而非事后的问题行为检测，并将此纳入对齐框架。

Result: 提出了一种新的研究使命：NLP应转向长期行为变化的测量，通过结合社会科学测量方法和NLP计算方法，不仅能理解人机交互的长期安全风险，还能引导模型开发向对用户产生积极而非消极结果的方向发展。

Conclusion: 建模用户行为随模型交互的转变能力，可以促进在线检测问题行为，并应被纳入对齐框架中以缓解用户的长期风险。

Abstract: Language models have taken on the role of a very new type of technology, by virtue of their "human-ness" and rapid integration into users' daily lives. This combination of features can introduce longitudinal risks---cognitive, developmental and socio-affective changes in humans---that might not surface in short-term interactions, but can have lasting long-term effects on users. This forms the basis of a critical new mission for NLP: to pivot from static, short-term evaluations of text generations to long-term measurements of behavioral changes, towards a diachronic understanding of human-model interactions. In this work, we draw from measurements used in social science fields that are crucial to understand emergent phenomena in longitudinal data. We discuss how computational methods in the field of NLP need to be combined with such measurements, not only to understand long-term safety risks of human-model interactions, but to help steer model development towards positive rather than negative outcomes for users. This ability to model human behavioral shifts as a function of model interactions can facilitate online rather than post-hoc detection of problematic behaviors, and should be leveraged in alignment frameworks to mitigate long-term risks in users.

</details>


### [227] [CMuon: Accelerating and Stabilizing Diffusion Transformer Training via Chunked Momentum Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02502)
*Chuyan Chen,Peng Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Chunked Muon (CMuon)优化器，通过将DiT中融合的张量按功能子组件拆分后再进行正交化，解决了vanilla Muon在DiT训练后期收敛不佳的问题，实现比AdamW快2倍以上的训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT训练计算成本高昂，Muon优化器虽作为AdamW的有力替代方案，但直接应用于DiT时后期收敛效果不佳。作者指出根本原因在于标准DiT架构将功能不同的权重（如AdaLN和QKV层）融合为统一张量，Muon对融合张量的正交化会无意中引入隐式子空间耦合，扭曲更新方向并损害全局优化。

Method: 提出Chunked Muon (CMuon)策略：在正交化之前，将融合矩阵按功能划分为独立的子组件（chunks），分别对每个子组件进行正交化处理，从而避免隐式子空间耦合问题。

Result: 使用CMuon训练的675M参数DiT模型在ImageNet 256上仅用200个epoch即达到FID 1.18，相比AdamW实现超过2倍的训练加速，同时有效克服了vanilla Muon的后期收敛平台期。

Conclusion: CMuon是一种简单但高效的优化策略，通过解决Muon在融合张量上的子空间耦合问题，显著加速DiT训练并改善收敛质量。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in visual generative modeling, yet their training remains computationally prohibitive. While the recently proposed Momentum Orthogonalization (Muon) optimizer offers a promising alternative to AdamW, its direct application to DiTs yields suboptimal late-stage convergence. In this paper, we identify the root cause of this bottleneck: standard DiT architectures fuse functionally distinct weights (e.g., within AdaLN and QKV layers) into unified tensors for computational efficiency. Applying Muon to these fused tensors inadvertently induces implicit subspace coupling, which distorts update directions and degrades global optimization. To address this, we introduce Chunked Muon (CMuon), a simple yet highly effective strategy that partitions these matrices into independent sub-components prior to orthogonalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a 675M-parameter DiT trained with CMuon achieves a FID of 1.18 on ImageNet 256 in just 200 epochs. This represents more than a 2x training speedup over AdamW, while effectively overcoming the late-stage convergence plateaus of vanilla Muon.

</details>


### [228] [Abduction Without a Body? Representational Grounding and the Abduction Loop for Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02505)
*Michael Farmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"身份溯因"（identity abduction）概念，认为科学溯因推理不一定需要连续的具身感知，而可通过表征转换暴露潜在不变量来实现。作者提出Abduction Loop架构和DAB-30基准测试，以图表为媒介实现跨学科对应关系的假设生成与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI和科学哲学领域中"真实假设生成必须依赖代理与物理世界的连续耦合"这一论点，作者希望论证在线具身并非所有溯因科学行为的前提条件，从而为无具身溯因推理开辟理论空间。

Method: 提出"惯例空间"（convention space）概念来解释科学图表如何部分规范化对称、拓扑和算子结构；设计Abduction Loop架构，包含表征生成、基序提取、惯例空间规范化、跨领域检索、身份假设生成和对抗验证六个环节，以弃权为默认行为。同时构建DAB-30基准作为可证伪的评估程序。

Result: 以一个具体案例作为"可能性见证"：多模态模型从引力记忆输运模型的图中生成并验证了其中心微分复形与弱引力透镜宇宙学中球面Kaiser-Squires质量映射复形等价的假设。作者明确此案例仅用于抽象架构，并非普遍能力的证据。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于一个机制性提议、一个架构和一个测试程序，主张通过表征落地（而非身体交互）获得新的推断性可供性，并给出了可证伪的评估路径。

Abstract: Can scientific abduction occur without continuous sensorimotor embodiment? Recent arguments in AI and philosophy of science hold that genuine hypothesis generation requires an agent continuously coupled to the physical world. We defend a narrower claim: online embodiment is not necessary for every abductive scientific act. Our focus is identity abduction: the inference that two independently developed structures are one object under an explicit correspondence, reached through representational grounding rather than bodily interaction. An agent may acquire new inferential affordances not through physical interaction but through transformations into representations that expose latent invariants. Scientific diagrams are a practical substrate because they embody independently evolved conventions that partially canonicalize symmetry, topology, and operator structure across disciplines - a property we develop as convention space, which answers a hard retrieval problem: finding mathematically related work when two fields share no discriminating vocabulary. We operationalize the mechanism as an architecture, the Abduction Loop: representation generation, motif extraction, convention-space canonicalization, cross-domain retrieval, identity-hypothesis generation, and adversarial verification, with abstention as the designed default. A documented episode, in which a multimodal model given a figure of a gravitational-memory transport model generated and then verified the hypothesis that its central differential complex is equivalent to the spherical Kaiser-Squires mass-mapping complex of weak-lensing cosmology, serves as a motivating possibility witness from which the architecture is abstracted, not as evidence of general capability. We close with a falsifiable evaluation program, the DAB-30 benchmark. The contribution is a mechanistic proposal, an architecture, and a test program.

</details>


### [229] [Magnet: Detecting Cross-Session AI Misuse Through Capability Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02518)
*Natalie Isak,Matthew Dressman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出最先进的AI系统是多个智能体协同工作的架构，但现有的滥用检测框架仅针对单轮或多轮（单会话）威胁模型，无法应对攻击者将有害目标分解为无害单元并在多个隔离的智能体会话中执行这一跨会话规避攻击。作者首先证明了跨会话目标分解作为一种规避技术的有效性，随后提出Magnet框架，通过在用户ID聚合级别建模跨会话、跨时间累积的相关能力，将分散在大量良性会话中的可疑证据吸引聚集为紧凑的证据包供检测器使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最强大的AI部署是多个专门智能体的组合架构，但现有滥用检测文献仅覆盖单会话威胁模型，忽略了攻击者可以将有害目标分解后在多个隔离会话中执行来规避检测的关键安全漏洞——智能体在会话间无状态，但攻击者有状态，这种不对称性使得跨会话攻击轨迹能有效逃避检测。

Method: 贡献分为两部分：其一，提出并演示跨会话目标分解（cross-session goal decomposition）作为规避技术，将单次谈话中可能被检测的有害目标拆解为多个无危害的单元在隔离会话中执行；其二，提出Magnet检测框架，在更高层级的关联器（如用户ID）上聚合跨会话、跨时间累积的能力证据，通过吸引机制将散落在大量良性会话中的关键证据（"needles"）聚集为紧凑的证据包供检测器推理，而非逐会话逐一检查。

Result: 实验表明跨会话分解攻击比等效的单会话或多轮攻击能够激发更多有害能力。Magnet框架能够高效且稳健地检测此类跨会话攻击，通过证据聚合而非逐会话穷举搜索来实现。

Conclusion: 现有单会话检测框架存在关键盲区，攻击者可以利用智能体会话间无状态而攻击者有状态的不对称性进行跨会话攻击规避。作者提出的Magnet框架通过跨会话能力聚合和证据吸引机制，为解决这类新型攻击提供了有效的检测思路。

Abstract: The most capable AI deployments are not single models but ensembles of specialized agents that delegate and act in coordination. This architecture unlocks powerful new capabilities, and it also introduces risks that existing frameworks for monitoring, detection, and mitigation were not designed to address. Most state-of-the-art AI abuse detection literature focuses on single-turn or multi-turn (single-session) threat models. This leaves a critical gap: an attacker can decompose a harmful goal into innocuous-looking units and execute each in isolated agentic sessions. The agent is stateless between conversations, but the attacker is not. This asymmetry allows for cross-session trajectories that are effective at evading detection. Our contributions are twofold. First, we demonstrate cross-session goal decomposition as an evasion technique, showing it may elicit more harmful capability than equivalent single-session or multi-turn attacks. By capability we mean an artifact produced at one step of an objective, evidenced by what an interaction produced (model responses and tool-call results), and composable with capabilities accrued elsewhere into a harmful whole. Second, we propose Magnet: an efficient and robust detection approach that models relevant capabilities accrued over time and across agentic conversations, aggregated at a higher-level correlator (in this case, a user ID) rather than per-conversation state. The main challenge is assembling the evidence bundle Magnet reasons over. The incriminating artifacts may be needles scattered through a haystack of benign sessions that are individually harmless, dangerous only once collected. Rather than searching the haystack straw-by-straw (i.e. per-session inspection), Magnet does what its name implies: it attracts the relevant needles out of the hay, across sessions and across time, into a compact evidence bundle a detector can act on.

</details>


### [230] [A Taxonomy of Cognitive Capability Gaps in Generative and Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02553)
*Taye Akinrele,Sindhuja Penchala,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分类法的认知AI综述，围绕五个维度（持久状态建模、目标导向自主性、自我监控与控制、环境交互、学习与适应）系统梳理了认知能力缺口，并提出了自适应认知智能架构（ACIA）和面向认知的评估框架，为通向AGI提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和智能体AI虽然表现出色，但许多基础认知功能仍然碎片化或发展薄弱，限制了系统在长时间跨度上的可靠运行。该论文旨在系统性地识别和归类这些认知能力缺口。

Method: 采用分类法驱动的综述方法，将文献组织为五个认知维度（持久状态建模、目标导向自主性、自我监控与控制、环境交互、学习与适应），对每个维度回顾近期进展、识别反复出现的局限并讨论开放研究挑战，在此基础上提出自适应认知智能架构（ACIA）和认知中心评估方向。

Result: 提出了一个统一的研究组织框架，涵盖分类法、架构视角和评估框架三部分，系统化了现有研究并明确了未解决的挑战，为认知AI的发展提供了路线图。

Conclusion: 该分类法、架构视角与评估框架共同为构建更可靠的长时推理、自适应决策和持续学习能力的AI系统奠定了基础，指明了通向认知AI乃至AGI的关键研究方向。

Abstract: Cognitive AI seeks to move beyond language generation and autonomous task execution toward systems capable of sustained reasoning, adaptive behavior, persistent memory, and self-regulation. While generative and agentic AI have demonstrated impressive capabilities across a wide range of tasks, many fundamental cognitive functions remain fragmented or weakly developed, limiting reliable operation over extended time horizons. This paper presents a taxonomy-driven survey of the major cognitive capability gaps that continue to constrain the development of Cognitive AI. The literature is organized around five dimensions: persistent state modeling, goal-directed autonomy, self-monitoring and control, environment interaction, and learning and adaptation. For each dimension, we review recent advances, identify recurring limitations, and discuss open research challenges. Building on these insights, we outline a conceptual Adaptive Cognitive Intelligence Architecture (ACIA) and examine emerging directions in cognition-centric evaluation. The proposed taxonomy provides a unified framework for organizing existing research, identifying unresolved challenges, and guiding the design of future cognitively capable systems. Together, the taxonomy, architectural perspective, and evaluation framework offer a roadmap for advancing AI systems that exhibit more reliable long-term reasoning, adaptive decision-making, and continual learning. The survey highlights key research opportunities toward more adaptive, reliable, and cognitively capable AI systems, providing a foundation for future progress toward Cognitive AI and, ultimately, Artificial General Intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [231] [AtumAI: A Principled Framework for Agentic Generation of Datacenter Control-Plane Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02569)
*Qiushi Lin,Chaojie Zhang,Íñigo Goiri,Aditya Akella,Ricardo Bianchini,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtumAI是一个利用智能体AI自动生成数据中心控制平面策略的框架，通过Datacenter Task Compiler将自然语言需求编译为形式化、可搜索的规范，再通过Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop（结合扩散模型、进化算法和代理模型）进行系统化搜索，在三个控制平面任务上均优于专家设计的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心控制平面策略设计日益困难：软硬件栈快速增长、设计空间庞大且相互依赖，单个策略的原型开发需要数月时间。现有Agentic AI在自动化此搜索时存在三大不足：缺乏形式化（无法利用问题结构、不保证硬约束）、不可迁移（每次从零开始）、不系统（仅依赖LLM探索狭窄设计空间，陷入局部最优）。

Method: AtumAI包含两个核心组件：(1) Datacenter Task Compiler：将自然语言描述的目标自动编译为形式化、机器可检查、可搜索的任务规范（包括目标、约束、决策变量和评估方法）；(2) Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop：通过扩散模型、进化算法和代理模型扩展搜索空间，超越LLM自身的候选生成能力，迭代提出、测试和优化候选策略直至满足需求。

Result: 在三个问题范围、设计空间和权衡不同的控制平面任务（工作负载放置、资源缩放、功耗管理）上评估，AtumAI生成的策略一致优于专家工程设计的基线。同时将新任务的接入时间从数月缩减到仅需编写任务描述。

Conclusion: AtumAI证明了通过形式化、可迁移和系统化的智能体AI框架，可以显著加速数据中心控制平面策略的自动化设计，将新任务的工程化周期从数月压缩至描述编写级别，并产出超越专家设计的策略。

Abstract: The efficiency of a datacenter rests on its control plane policies. Designing these policies is increasingly hard: the hardware-software stack grows fast, the design space is vast and interdependent, and prototyping a single policy takes months. Agentic AI promises to automate this search. Off the shelf, however, it falls short on three fronts. It is not formal: with no structured, searchable statement of the problem, the search has little structure to exploit and hard constraints are not guaranteed. It is not transferable: each task is solved from scratch, so nothing learned on one task carries to the next. Finally, it is not systematic: relying on the LLM as the sole source of candidates, it explores a narrow slice of the design space and settles into local optima. We introduce AtumAI, a framework that generates datacenter control-plane policies with agentic AI, making the process formal, transferable, and systematic. From a goal stated in plain language, AtumAI autonomously proposes, tests, and refines candidate policies until one satisfies the request. It does so through two components. The Datacenter Task Compiler automates problem formulation: it compiles the request into a formal, machine-checkable, and searchable specification of the task's objectives, constraints, decision variables, and evaluation methodology. The Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop then searches this specification, expanding the search beyond the LLM itself via a diffusion model, an evolutionary algorithm, and a surrogate model. Together, they reduce onboarding a new task from months of engineering to writing its description. We evaluate AtumAI on three control-plane tasks with distinct problem scopes, design spaces, and trade-offs: workload placement, resource scaling, and power management. Across all tasks, the policies generated by AtumAI consistently outperform expert-engineered baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Uncertainty-Aware Simulation-Based Inference for Operations Research with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00019)
*Liang Guo,Lin Shaochong,Shen Zuo-Jun Max,Zhang Kun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的、不确定性感知的推理框架，用于提升大语言模型在运筹学数学建模任务中的可靠性。该方法通过前瞻性模拟评估中间候选步骤的下游预测不确定性，并利用重要性重采样选择更可能生成连贯数学模型的候选方案，在多个OR基准上优于标准基线和低温基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在运筹学任务中的部署面临挑战，因为正确性依赖于连贯的建模过程而非仅仅正确的最终答案。标准的自回归生成采用短视策略，无法预判局部分式是否能有效扩展为全局一致的优化模型，导致局部看似合理的步骤可能传播为灾难性的下游建模或求解器代码错误。

Method: 提出一种不确定性感知、无需训练的推理框架。不更新模型参数，而是利用短前瞻模拟评估中间候选步骤，量化下游预测不确定性或概率集中度。通过重要性重采样动态选择更可能产生连贯数学表述的候选。

Result: 在NL4OPT、MAMO和IndustryOR等多个OR基准上的实证评估表明，该框架一致性地优于标准和低温基线，建立了高效且无需训练的可靠OR公式生成范式。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了无需更新模型参数即可有效提升LLM在OR建模任务中可靠性的可行性，为可靠OR公式生成提供了一种高效、训练自由的推理范式。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) for operations research (OR) tasks remains challenging because correctness depends on a coherent modeling process, not merely a correct final answer. Standard autoregressive generation operates on a myopic policy, which sometimes fails to anticipate whether a partial formulation can be validly extended into a globally consistent optimization model. Consequently, locally plausible steps may propagate into catastrophic downstream formulation or solver code errors. To address this, we propose an uncertainty-aware, training-free inference framework for OR mathematical modeling. Without updating model parameters, our method evaluates intermediate candidate steps using short lookahead simulations to quantify downstream predictive uncertainty or probability concentration. Candidates that demonstrate a higher likelihood of yielding coherent mathematical formulations are then dynamically selected via importance resampling. Empirical evaluations across multiple OR benchmarks (including NL4OPT, MAMO, and IndustryOR) demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms both standard and low-temperature baselines, establishing an efficient, training-free paradigm for reliable OR formulation generation.

</details>


### [233] [Learning Compositional Meta-Routing for Agentic Workflows: An Executable Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00106)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可执行的智能体路由基准测试和一个预算感知的元路由器，该路由器从原始任务文本中组合异构操作（直接回答、分解请求、检索、执行代码、委派、验证）。在保留测试集上学习策略达到100%成功率（静态基线为93.5%），成本降低43%；但在词汇偏移挑战集上降至75.9%，表明词汇泛化是主要瓶颈而非路由执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由工作往往孤立地选择模型端点、检索深度或工具，缺乏一个可执行、可机器验证的基准来评估智能体系统如何根据任务文本组合多种异构操作（推理和执行步骤），并考虑预算约束下的路由决策。

Method: 构建包含216训练/72开发/108测试/108词汇偏移挑战任务的可执行基准，覆盖数据分析、冻结语料研究和文档处理。采用独立的正则化逻辑回归头从词和字符特征预测操作概率，在开发数据上做温度缩放，并在路由成本和动作计数预算下进行贪心组合。

Result: 在保留测试集上，学习策略达到100%成功率（静态和固定工作流为93.5%），成本降低43%；匹配的学习一步式路由仅达56.5%。在未触碰的词汇偏移挑战集上，学习成功率降至75.9%，落后于静态路由的93.5%，但成本仍低49%，且超过一步式路由34.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果确认词汇泛化而非路由执行是当前系统的主要局限性。该工作建立了可复现的测试平台和有界的概念验证，但并不声称代表实时大语言模型的性能。

Abstract: Agentic systems must decide not only what answer to produce, but which reasoning and execution operations should precede it. A controller may answer directly, decompose a request, retrieve evidence, execute code, delegate to a specialist, or verify an intermediate result. Existing routing work largely selects model endpoints, retrieval depth, or tools in isolation. We introduce an executable benchmark and a budget-aware meta-router that composes heterogeneous operations from raw task text. The benchmark contains 216 training, 72 development, 108 held-out test, and 108 locked lexical-shift challenge tasks across data analysis, frozen-corpus research, and document processing. Outcomes are machine checked after operations execute. Independent regularized logistic heads predict operation probabilities from word and character features, are temperature-scaled on development data, and are greedily composed under route-cost and action-count budgets. On the held-out test, the learned policy achieves 100% success versus 93.5% for strong static and fixed workflows, with 43% lower cost than the static policy; a matched learned one-shot router reaches 56.5%. On the untouched challenge split, learned success falls to 75.9% and trails static routing at 93.5%, while remaining 49% cheaper and exceeding one-shot routing by 34.3 points. The gap identifies lexical generalization, rather than route execution, as the principal limitation. These results establish a reproducible testbed and a bounded proof of concept, not evidence of live-LLM performance.

</details>


### [234] [MetaRoute-Bench: Evaluating Meta-Decision Policies for Agentic Workflow Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00107)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaRoute-Bench是一个开放、可检查的框架，用于在共享执行模型下比较智能体系统的元决策策略（路由策略）。基准包含180个合成任务、8种路由策略和30对随机种子，共生成43,200条轨迹。实验表明任务感知的组合策略达到79.4%的成功率，优于静态策略（76.7%）、一次性任务路由（67.4%）和直接回答（52.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统频繁需要做出元决策（直接回答、分解任务、调用工具、执行代码、委托专家、验证中间结果或从失败中恢复），这些决策影响任务成功率、成本和延迟，但通常被嵌入编排框架中且仅通过聚合任务准确率评估，缺乏系统性的比较方法。

Method: 构建MetaRoute-Bench基准框架，包含180个覆盖数据分析、研究和文档处理的合成任务配置、8种路由策略以及30对配对随机种子，通过离线执行模型生成43,200条轨迹，对路由策略进行系统比较和消融分析。

Result: 任务感知组合策略达到79.4%成功率，优于工作负载特定的静态策略（76.7%）、一次性任务路由（67.4%）和直接回答（52.9%）。组合策略相比静态策略提升2.7个百分点（配对95% CI为±2.0），但平均成本增加4.7%、延迟增加6.4%。消融实验显示限制为单一操作或移除验证环节时性能损失最大。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要贡献是可复现的评估方法和路由策略权衡分析，而非生产环境有效性证据。作者发布任务生成、策略、轨迹、测试和分析工件以支持实时系统验证。

Abstract: Agentic systems must repeatedly decide whether to answer directly, decompose a task, invoke a tool, execute code, delegate to a specialist, verify an intermediate result, or recover from failure. These meta-decisions affect not only task success but also operating cost and latency, yet they are often embedded inside an orchestration framework and evaluated only through aggregate task accuracy. We present MetaRoute-Bench, an open, inspectable framework for comparing meta-decision policies under a shared execution model. The initial benchmark contains 180 synthetic task profiles spanning data analysis, research, and document processing, eight routing policies, and 30 paired random seeds. Across 43,200 traces, a task-aware compositional policy achieves 79.4% success compared with 76.7% for a strong workload-specific static policy, 67.4% for one-shot task routing, and 52.9% for direct answering. Relative to the static policy, this is a 2.7 percentage-point improvement with paired 95% CI of plus or minus 2.0 points, at 4.7% higher mean cost and 6.4% higher latency. Ablations show the largest losses when route composition is restricted to one operation and when verification is removed. These results are generated by a seeded offline execution model rather than a live deployment; accordingly, the primary contribution is a reproducible evaluation method and an analysis of routing-policy tradeoffs, not evidence of production effectiveness. We release task generation, policies, traces, tests, and analysis artifacts to support live-system validation.

</details>


### [235] [Progressive$^2$: A Teacher-Student Progressive Co-Evolving Knowledge Distillation Method for Substantial Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00129)
*Tiancong Cheng,Ying Zhang,Zhiwen Yu,Yifang Yin,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Progressive²蒸馏框架，通过渐进增强教师模型（按语义层次逐步选择更多层参与蒸馏）和渐进缩小学生模型（逐步减小网络规模实现迭代共同进化）的组合，解决教师与学生能力差距过大时知识蒸馏性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在服务器模型压缩中广泛应用，但当服务器模型能力与客户端需求之间存在较大差距时，传统蒸馏方法的性能会受到严重影响，需要一种更有效的渐进式蒸馏策略来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 1) 教师侧采用渐进式策略，按照从原始到丰富的语义进展顺序逐步增加参与蒸馏的层数，构建系统化的学习课程；2) 设计基于Lipschitz连续性的多特征融合适配器提升训练稳定性；3) 学生侧采用渐进缩小网络规模的方式，与教师实现迭代共同进化；4) 整个框架灵活，教师侧策略可独立部署或与学生侧联合使用。

Result: Progressive²模型既能独立部署教师渐进策略以实现准确率与训练效率的最优平衡，也能通过师生联合集成进一步提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 渐进式蒸馏策略（包含教师端逐步加深和学生端逐步缩小）可以有效缓解师生能力差距过大导致的蒸馏性能下降问题，且该框架具有较好的灵活性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a widely utilized technique for transferring knowledge from a large model (the teacher) to a smaller model (the student). Owing to its flexibility and broad applicability, KD has been extensively applied in the compression of server-side models to meet the Quality of Service (QoS) requirements of client users. Despite significant advancements, the performance of distillation is substantially compromised when a large disparity exists between the capabilities of the server and the requirements of the client. To alleviate this problem, we propose a novel distillation approach, named Progressive$^2$, which operates through the combination of a progressively stronger teacher and a progressively smaller student. On the side of the teacher, rather than involving all layers simultaneously, we progressively select additional layers for distillation following a raw-to-rich semantic progression, establishing a systematic learning curriculum. Furthermore, we design a teacher-side multi-feature fusion adapter for the teacher to improve training stability, which is theoretically supported by the framework of Lipschitz continuity. On the side of the student, rather than directly training a tiny model, we gradually reduce the size of the network to facilitate an iterative co-evolution with the teacher. Progressive$^2$ serves as a flexible framework; the progressive strategy of the teacher can be deployed independently to achieve an optimal balance between accuracy and training efficiency, while the joint integration of the teacher and the student yields further improvements in overall performance.

</details>


### [236] [Rethinking Pretraining for Specialized Design Data: Evidence from the JONES-19 Cultural Design Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00135)
*Alexandros Haridis,Charles Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于JONES-19建筑设计图像数据集，比较了CNN在ImageNet预训练与从头训练两种策略下的判别性能，发现局部多尺度采样可从零学习有效逼近预训练效果，表明在专业设计领域，小型高质量数据集比大规模通用预训练更具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计与建筑档案以图形格式编码专家知识，为设计启发的机器学习提供了独特的测试平台。现有研究缺乏对小型专业设计数据集中CNN学习策略的系统评估，特别是预训练与从头训练之间的性能差异及其机制尚不明确。

Method: 基于JONES-19数据集（源自1857年出版的《装饰语法》），评估CNN在两种训练策略下的判别性能：（a）ImageNet预训练迁移学习，提供领域通用的"视觉常识"；（b）在JONES-19设计数据上从头训练，并结合重复局部采样（多裁剪）增强策略。

Result: 领域通用预训练先验可提升判别性能，但从头训练结合多裁剪局部采样能够有效弥补这一差距。对于高度结构化的设计数据，局部设计驱动的表征足以提供学习基础。

Conclusion: 在专业设计领域中，精心策划的小型高质量数据集（捕捉经验性与形式化设计原则）可能比追求大规模数据收集更为有效且更能揭示特定设计领域的本质。

Abstract: Design and architectural archives encode expert human knowledge in graphical formats, providing a critical testbed for design-inspired Machine Learning (ML) challenges absent with typical computer vision benchmarks. Building on JONES-19, a small-size image dataset based on The Grammar of Ornament (London, 1857), we evaluate the discriminative performance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in two model training strategies: (a) ImageNet pretraining for domain-general "visual common sense," and (b) learning from scratch on the design data in JONES-19. We find that while domain-general priors improve discriminative performance, learning from scratch augmented with repeated local sampling (multi-crop) effectively recovers these gains. For highly structured design data, local design-driven representations provide sufficient foundation for learning, challenging a reliance on massive general-purpose pretraining. These findings suggest that in specialized design domains, careful curation of smaller high-quality datasets that capture empirical and formal design principles may prove more effective and informative on the nature of a particular design domain than prioritizing large-scale data collection.

</details>


### [237] [Leak It: A Probabilistic Approach to Training-Data Extraction from Black-Box Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00144)
*Victor Maricato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Membership inference (MIA) on language models is usually summarised by an aggregate ROC-AUC, but such evaluations are confounded: model-free blind baselines separate members from non-members from surface text alone. We study black-box, sampling-based training-data leakage through a probabilistic lens, treating N samples from p(.|x) as an estimate of the output distribution and casting leakage signals as functionals of it. We extend the blind-baseline critique into the sampling regime: on WikiMIA a blind bag-of-words classifier reaches AUC 0.97 (TPR 0.90 at 5% FPR) and sampling adds nothing, while on an IID Pile split (MIMIR) neither self-concentration nor gold-continuation recovery significantly beats a blind baseline (incremental AUC 95% CI includes zero). Aggregate metrics hide the real harm. The same sampling verbatim-extracts training data for a tail of documents no blind attack can reach. On Pythia-6.9B, 83 of 500 Pile documents bearing a real identifier (16.6%; 21.3% of those bearing an email address) have that exact identifier reproduced AND not reproduced under a mismatched-prefix control, so each leak is attributable to that document, not to a globally common string. This per-document disclosure is invisible to aggregate AUC and grows with capacity (5.6% to 16.6% from 410M to 6.9B). The risk is uneven: identifier leakage is ~3x stronger in code than prose, though prose stays clearly positive and also grows with capacity (4.0% to 12.1%), while recovery of arbitrary held-out continuations is confined to code (+0.44 member gap on GitHub vs at most +0.014 on prose). Temperature and nucleus sampling matter little, a 16-token prefix suffices, and we detect no reduction from corpus deduplication. Privacy audits should report per-document extraction, decomposed by domain, not a single AUC. We release leakit, a black-box extraction-audit tool.

</details>


### [238] [Response Magnitude as a Dominant Signal for Held-Out CRISPRi Perturbation Effect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00152)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究CRISPRi扰动转录组效应预测问题，发现目标基因的Anderson-Darling距离主要由四个确定性标量函数驱动，简单的四标量线性回归即可超越深度编码器，且幅度预测器在跨细胞类型迁移中表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测CRISPRi扰动对目标基因的转录组效应是单细胞生物学中的重要开放问题，但近期研究发现简单基线常能匹敌或超越深度预测模型，作者希望探究这一现象背后的低维信号及其跨细胞类型迁移特性。

Method: 在Virtual Cell Challenge基准上采用严格的目标基因held-out划分，识别驱动预测差距的低维信号（四个确定性标量函数）；比较深度MLP编码器、线性回归、随机森林等模型；通过两个预设对照区分排列对齐与维度增加的影响；并在两个外部CRISPRi筛选数据集上进行零样本迁移评估。

Result: 四个幅度标量的线性回归超越了最强的x-only经典模型；随机森林在输入+四标量上大幅超越深度编码器；幅度预测器零样本迁移为正，而表达预测器迁移为负或无法解析；将幅度暴露给深度编码器可改善迁移但未超过四标量线性回归；Anderson-Darling列实际衡量转录组广谱响应而非目标基因效应强度。

Conclusion: CRISPRi扰动预测任务中的关键可预测信号是低维的幅度标量而非高维输入本身，简单线性模型在该基准上优于深度方法；迁移评估需注意端点定义与实际测量含义的匹配问题。

Abstract: Predicting the magnitude of a CRISPRi perturbation's transcriptomic effect on held-out target genes is an important open problem in single-cell biology. Recent work has documented that simple baselines often match or exceed deep perturbation predictors on related protocols. We study this phenomenon on the Virtual Cell Challenge (VCC) benchmark under a strict held-out target-gene split, identify the specific low-dimensional signal that drives the gap, and characterize how it transfers across cell types. The target is the log Anderson-Darling distance from non-targeting controls, which is strongly predictable from four deterministic scalar functions of the 2,000-dimensional input. A deep MLP encoder with direct access to the full input collapses toward the marginal training mean, and standard remedies do not close the gap. A linear regression on the four magnitude scalars alone exceeds the strongest x-only classical model, while a Random Forest on the input plus the four scalars substantially outperforms our deep proof-of-concept encoder. Two pre-specified controls attribute the magnitude gain to per-row alignment rather than added dimensionality. Under zero-shot transfer to two external CRISPRi screens evaluated against a target-gene endpoint rebuilt from single-cell data, magnitude-only predictors transfer positively whereas expression-only predictors are negative or unresolved. Exposing magnitude to the deep encoder improves transfer over its expression-only counterpart, yet the encoder does not outperform a four-scalar linear regression on the same features. We also find that the Anderson-Darling column distributed with these screens measures transcriptome-wide response breadth rather than target-gene effect strength, so evaluating transfer against it scores a different outcome.

</details>


### [239] [Inference-Time Policy Alignment for Fair Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00175)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Conor Wallace,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推理时策略塑形框架，通过在推理阶段调整动作概率来实现基于福利的公平性对齐，无需更新预训练深度强化学习策略的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习智能体在部署后策略僵化，难以适应新的利益相关者偏好（如公平性）。已有的公平性强化学习方法通常假设公平性偏好预先已知，且需要完全重新训练策略，成本高昂。

Method: 借鉴大语言模型中的推理时对齐思想，将推理时公平性对齐形式化为策略塑形问题，提出乘性策略塑形框架，利用动作相关的福利分数调整动作概率，不需修改基础策略参数，且与任意深度强化学习智能体兼容。

Result: 在多个领域的广泛实验中，推理时策略塑形在显著提升基于福利的公平性目标的同时，保持了核心任务性能。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种高效、通用的推理时公平性对齐方法，避免了重新训练深度强化学习策略的高昂成本，为强化学习系统的适应性部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents achieve strong performance by optimizing scalar reward functions. However, once deployed, the policies of these RL agents are often rigid and costly to adapt to new performance criteria. For instance, an agent trained to maximize expected cumulative reward may not accommodate previously unknown stakeholder preferences. Existing approaches to achieve fairness, a type of preference, in RL typically assume that such preferences are known a priori and require complete retraining of the policy under a fairness-oriented metric. Inspired by inference-time alignment in large language models, we investigate the problem of steering a pretrained RL policy toward welfare-based fairness objectives at inference time without updating the base policy's parameters. We formalize inference-time fairness alignment as a policy shaping problem and propose a multiplicative policy shaping framework that adjusts action probabilities using action-dependent welfare scores, thus requiring no modification to the base policy. Our framework is general and compatible with any deep RL agent. Through extensive experiments across multiple domains, we demonstrate that inference-time policy shaping substantially improves welfare-based fairness objectives while preserving core task performance.

</details>


### [240] [AutoCause: A Python framework that automates expert decisions in environmental time-series causal discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00198)
*Marco Ruiz,Miguel Arana-Catania,David R. Ardila,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoCause是一个开源Python工作流，通过记录决策、提供默认参数和允许领域知识覆盖，将环境时间序列因果发现的专家决策过程标准化为可审计、可重复的分析流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境时间序列因果发现中，方法选择、条件独立性检验、滞后范围、样本量、多重检验控制等专家决策在不同数据集上应用不一致，导致生成的图无法比较、复现和审计。

Method: AutoCause工作流记录每个决策，从扩展的因果审计模块衍生默认参数，并支持领域知情覆盖。封装了三个家族中的四种成熟因果发现方法，添加非因果参考模型，并基于方法计数支持度对链接进行分级。

Result: 在来自DGP-Atlas、TimeGraph和基于拓扑的CausalRivers参考的145个数据集上，各方法恢复了参考图的不同互补部分。在合成基准上，多数支持的链接比单一方法链接更精确，但在河流拓扑上情况并非如此。

Conclusion: AutoCause将不一致的专家实践转化为可审计、可重复的分析，但因果解释仍由分析人员负责。

Abstract: Environmental time-series causal discovery requires expert decisions about method choice, conditional-independence tests, lag horizons, sample-size adequacy, multiple-testing control, and evidence interpretation. Applied inconsistently across datasets, these choices yield graphs that cannot be compared, reproduced, or audited. We present AutoCause, an open-source Python workflow that records each decision, derives defaults from an extended causal-audit module, and admits domain-informed overrides. The workflow wraps four established causal-discovery methods from three families, adds non-causal reference models, and grades links by method-count support. On 145 datasets from DGP-Atlas, TimeGraph, and a topology-derived CausalRivers reference, the methods recover complementary parts of the reference graphs. Majority-supported links are more precise than single-method links on the synthetic benchmarks but not against river topology. AutoCause converts inconsistent expert practice into an auditable, repeatable analysis; causal interpretation remains with the analyst. Available at https://github.com/marcoruizrueda/autocause.

</details>


### [241] [A Physics-Chemistry-Informed Neural Network (PCINN) for Real-Time Spatial-ALD Coverage Prediction and Reliable Kinetics Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00212)
*Ning Hu,Chang Liu,Yunlei Jiang,Yuan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理化学信息神经网络（PCINN）作为空间原子层沉积（SALD）的混合替代模型，在保持CFD级精度的同时实现实时预测（约7毫秒，比CFD快5×10^4倍），仅需30个训练样本即可达到R²=0.998。网络架构通过硬编码表面动力学层与非黑盒设计保持可解释性，并完成了完整的可辨识性分析（Fisher信息、剖面似然），揭示了吸附能与解吸速率可稳健辨识，而吸附速率仅在单一温度下不可单独辨识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SALD是工业原子层沉积的重要技术路线，但其设计和控制受限于表面覆盖率的预测成本：高保真CFD仿真太慢无法用于操作窗口扫描，而解析模型又无法捕捉如气帘等传输调制效应，亟需一种兼具精度与速度的替代模型。

Method: 构建PCINN混合代理模型：小型神经网络仅学习操作条件到近壁浓度的闭合关系，已知的表面动力学作为硬编码可训练化学层沿基底轨迹积分。整个架构形成单一标量瓶颈，保证稀疏数据下的准确性与可解释性。同时采用Fisher信息和剖面似然进行参数可辨识性分析，并推导出退化谷斜率k_B T_eff ln(10)作为可靠性诊断工具。

Result: PCINN在约7毫秒内返回覆盖率结果，比CFD快约5×10^4倍，测试R²_log=0.998（留一法R²_raw=0.974），仅需30个训练案例覆盖四个数量级的覆盖率范围。可辨识性分析发现E_ads和k_des可稳健辨识，k_ads在单一温度下不单独可辨识；七化学失配矩阵验证了斜率诊断的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PCINN成功弥合了CFD精度与实时速度之间的鸿沟，为非黑盒的物理化学信息替代建模提供了范例。研究所揭示的退化谷诊断工具可有效识别未建模的位点异质性，虽然数据来源于已知真实值的仿真，验证的是流程自洽性与可辨识性边界而非真实参数。

Abstract: Spatial atomic layer deposition (SALD) is a leading atmospheric-pressure, high-throughput route to industrial ALD, but design and control are limited by the cost of predicting surface coverage: high-fidelity CFD is far too slow for operating-window scans, while analytic models miss transport modulation such as the gas curtain. We present a physics-chemistry-informed neural network (PCINN), a hybrid surrogate with CFD-level accuracy at real-time speed: a query returns coverage in about 7 ms, roughly 5x10^4 times faster than a CFD solve, reaching a test R^2_log = 0.998 (leave-one-out R^2_raw = 0.974) from only 30 training cases spanning four orders of magnitude in coverage.
  The architecture is not a black box: a small network learns only the operating-condition to near-wall concentration closure, while the known surface kinetics is a hard-coded, trainable chemistry layer integrated along the substrate trajectory. This single-scalar bottleneck keeps it accurate under sparse data, interpretable and invertible.
  We add a full identifiability analysis (Fisher information, profile likelihood). The adsorption energy E_ads and desorption rate k_des are robustly identifiable; k_ads is not separately identifiable at a single temperature (only k_ads*c_wall is). Across four temperatures the prefactor nu and E_ads bind along a weakly identifiable degeneracy valley of slope 0.065 eV/decade, derived analytically as k_B T_eff ln(10) and turned into a reliability diagnostic: a seven-chemistry mismatch matrix shows it is invariant under any single-Arrhenius mismatch and shifts only when a second thermally activated process appears, so a slope departure flags unmodelled site heterogeneity.
  Data come from simulation with known ground truth inverted by the same kinetic form, so the study verifies pipeline self-consistency and the identifiability boundary, not real parameters.

</details>


### [242] [Verifier-Induced Support Reshaping in On-Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00220)
*Shaohang Wei,Zikun Su,Feifan Song,Wen Luo,Wei Li,Guangyue Peng,Houfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于可验证奖励的on-policy RL（RLVR）在提升当前任务目标的同时，可能使后续任务的成功行为变得过于稀少而无法采样和强化，作者称之为"验证器诱导的支持重塑"（verifier-induced support reshaping）。实验表明Math-RLVR提升了指令遵循的平均成功率但降低了重复采样下任何成功响应的提示数量，而IF-RLVR则使数学响应从逐步推理转向直接作答并降低best@k。这些变化集中在响应的前几个token，RLVR主要重排基础策略中已有的开头，而选定的开头因果性地影响数学可搜索性。参考文献约束、路由先验和on-policy蒸馏只能部分保持跨任务支持，端点改进不能保证未来的可训练性或联合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR研究主要关注单一任务的端点性能提升，忽视了on-policy优化过程中成功轨迹支持集（support set）的重新塑造可能对后续任务训练带来的负面影响。作者希望揭示RLVR在提升当前目标的同时，是否会让后续学习变得更困难。

Method: 作者通过重复验证器评分采样和双向训练（数学推理和受约束指令遵循），在两种模型家族上系统研究RLVR效应。具体包括：Math-RLVR与IF-RLVR的顺序训练、token分布分析、受控开头干预实验，以及测试参考策略约束、路由先验和on-policy蒸馏等方法。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B-Base上，Math-RLVR使IFEval pass@1提升6.5个百分点但best@32下降9.8个百分点；同一分歧在两种模型和两个IF基准上均出现。IF-RLVR使数学响应从逐步推理偏向直接答案，降低各采样预算下的best@k，并减少后续Math-RLVR的奖励变化。改动集中在响应的前几个token，RLVR主要重排已有开头，开头选择因果影响数学可搜索性。现有约束方法只能部分保持跨任务支持。

Conclusion: on-policy RLVR的端点改进不能保证未来的可训练性或联合能力。验证器诱导的支持重塑是一个需要被显式理解和管理的现象，当前的方法（参考策略约束、路由先验、on-policy蒸馏）只能部分缓解这一问题。

Abstract: We show that on-policy reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can improve the current objective while making successful behaviors for later objectives too rare to sample and reinforce. We call this verifier-induced support reshaping and define effective rewardable support as successful trajectories reachable within a fixed rollout budget. Across two model families, we study this effect through repeated verifier-scored sampling and bidirectional training on mathematical reasoning and constrained instruction following, including sequential training with the opposite verifier. Math-RLVR raises average instruction-following success but reduces the number of prompts with any successful response under repeated sampling. On IFEval with Qwen3-8B-Base, pass@1 rises by 6.5 percentage points while best@32 falls by 9.8 percentage points, and the same divergence appears across both models and IF benchmarks. Conversely, IF-RLVR shifts math responses from step-by-step openings toward direct answers, lowers best@k across sampling budgets, and reduces reward variation for later Math-RLVR. Token-distribution analyses and controlled opening interventions show that these changes concentrate in the first few response tokens. RLVR mainly reranks openings already available in the base policy, and the selected opening causally affects math searchability. The tested reference-policy constraints, routing priors, and on-policy distillation preserve cross-task support only partially; MathIF and ReasonIF show that marginal gains translate only partly into responses that are both correct and constraint-following. Therefore, endpoint improvements do not guarantee future trainability or joint capability under on-policy optimization. Code is available at https://github.com/sylvain-wei/verifier-induced-support-reshaping

</details>


### [243] [Similarity-Aware Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00246)
*Madhavan Citalamangalam Kumaran,Midhun Parakkal Unni,Vicky Kouni,Haripriya Harikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保留感知（retain-aware）的定位式机器遗忘方法，在参数选择时同时考虑被遗忘数据和保留数据的重要性，并构建了基于余弦相似度的保留相似评估集来直接衡量附带损害。在CIFAR-10和ResNet18上的11种实验设置中，该方法在降低附带损害的同时提升了标准遗忘指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于定位的机器遗忘方法仅根据遗忘集的重要性选择参数，忽视了这些参数在保留数据集中的作用，导致对语义相似的保留样本造成附带损害。

Method: 提出保留感知的定位方法，综合考虑参数对遗忘数据和保留数据两者的重要性进行参数选择；同时引入基于模型嵌入空间余弦相似度构建的保留相似评估集，用于直接度量附带损害。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集和ResNet18模型上的11种实验设置中，该方法一致地降低了附带损害，同时改善了标准遗忘评估指标。

Conclusion: 保留感知的定位方法能有效提升相似感知的机器遗忘效果，在减少对保留数据附带损害的同时保持甚至提升遗忘性能。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of user-specified training examples from a trained model, avoiding the need to retrain it from scratch. Localization-based methods improve unlearning efficiency by identifying a subset of influential model parameters. However, existing approaches select parameters based solely on forget-set importance, neglecting their role in retained dataset and often causing collateral damage to semantically similar retained examples. We address this limitation with a retain-aware localization method that considers parameter importance to both forgotten and retained data. We also introduce a retain-similar evaluation set, constructed using cosine similarity in the model embedding space, to directly measure collateral damage. Across eleven experimental settings on CIFAR-10 dataset and ResNet18 model, our method consistently reduces collateral damage while improving standard unlearning metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of retain-aware localization for similarity-aware machine unlearning.

</details>


### [244] [Stabilized Best-of-$K$ Training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00296)
*Melveena Jolly,Midhun Xavier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Leader Reward训练方法进行窄范围扩展测试，将二元领导/非领导区分替换为基于采样预算K的稳定化排名信号。在固定TSP-100测试集和POMO架构下，发现稳定化K=8的方案在独立采样场景中比原始Leader Reward更优（Best-of-8成本7.7944 vs 7.8136），但作者明确声明该结果仅是估计层面的、解码器特定的观察，不构成普遍优越性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Leader Reward训练方法中二元区分信号的潜在改进空间，通过引入稳定化的排名信号（由采样预算K索引）来替代原有的二元领导/非领导判别，以观察是否能进一步提升组合优化任务的解决方案质量。

Method: 在POMO架构上，保持3,050轮训练计划和TSP-100测试集固定，将Leader Reward的二元区分替换为基于采样预算K的稳定化排名信号。利用三个配对的训练种子，在独立采样模式下比较Best-of-8成本，同时验证原始实现的100-start、8-augmentation贪婪解码性能。

Result: Leader Reward复现结果在100-start、8-augmentation贪婪解码下为7.7662，与报告值7.766一致。在独立采样场景中，稳定化K=8的配方在三个配对种子上均优于原始Leader Reward（7.7944 vs 7.8136）。但Leader Reward在采样K=1时更优，且在其原始增强贪婪协议下仍略占优势。

Conclusion: 稳定化排名信号在独立采样且K=8的特定设置下展现出一定优势，但该结论受限于解码器类型和采样预算，不具有普遍性。由于种子数量（3个）低于六种子测试下限，且Leader Reward在多数其他设置下表现更好，因此不做无偏估计器、普遍优越性或最先进结果的声明。

Abstract: Leader Reward modifies POMO training to emphasize the best trajectory produced by repeated inference. We test a narrow extension: replace its binary leader/non-leader distinction with a stabilized rank signal indexed by a sampling budget $K$. With the POMO architecture, 3,050-epoch schedule, and TSP-100 test set held fixed, the Leader Reward reimplementation obtains $7.7662$ under 100-start, 8-augmentation greedy decoding, matching the reported $7.766$ at its displayed precision. Under independent sampling, the stabilized $K=8$ recipe lowers realized Best-of-8 cost in all three paired training seeds: $7.7944$ versus $7.8136$. This observation is estimation-only and decoder-specific: three seeds are below the six-seed testing floor, Leader Reward is better at sampled $K=1$, and it remains slightly better under its original augmented-greedy protocol. We make no unbiased-estimator, universal superiority, or state-of-the-art claim.

</details>


### [245] [Abstention as an Action Can Kill Both the Reward Gradient and the KL Anchor: Collapse Law and Repair for Error-Penalized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00301)
*Xujun Che,Yuchen Yuan,Weida Zhao,Chenyang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于KL锚定的梯度学习器在面对错误惩罚评分规则时，可能产生与理性代理相反的行为——模型会漂移向拒绝回答一切，同时训练奖励看似提升（趋近于零），实则是覆盖率崩溃。修复方案是训练一个强制置信度报告并使用严格适当的评分加正确性奖励，仅在部署时通过阈值报告来弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误惩罚评分规则（答对+1，答错-λ，弃权0）被越来越多地用于对抗幻觉，理论上理性代理会在正确概率超过Chow阈值时回答。但作者发现这样的规则在梯度学习场景下可能适得其反，导致模型系统性地放弃回答。

Method: 作者通过理论分析证明，当弃权是离散动作时，奖励梯度和锚定恢复力被同一个门饱和因子削弱并同时消亡。在明确条件下（全面作答在期望上失分、提示有界读出），模型漂移向全拒绝状态。还分析了优势估计器在稀疏回答机制下将设计惩罚替换为有效惩罚1，使学习阈值从t*变为1/2。提出的修复方案是训练强制置信度报告加严格适当评分与正确性奖励，部署时对报告设阈值来弃权。

Result: 模拟验证了所有理论预测；在两个规模的语言模型实验确认了机制：规则在十步优化器内让模型沉默于其仍能解决的问题，消融实验隔离了原因，报告级训练同时提升了覆盖率、准确率和校准度。

Conclusion: 离散弃权动作下的错误惩罚评分规则对梯度学习器可能产生与设计意图相反的效果；结构性修复（强制置信度报告训练+部署时阈值化弃权）是有效方案，因为报告总是发射、没有门饱和，奖励梯度和锚定不会同时消亡，校准最优是吸引子。

Abstract: Error-penalized scoring rules ($+1$ for a correct answer, $-λ$ for a wrong one, $0$ for abstaining) are increasingly prescribed against hallucination: a rational agent facing such a rule answers exactly when its correctness probability exceeds Chow's threshold $t^\ast=λ/(1+λ)$. We prove that a KL-anchored gradient learner can do the opposite. When abstention is a discrete action, the reward gradient and the anchor's restoring force are throttled by the same gate-saturation factor and die together: under explicit conditions (among them, blanket answering loses score in expectation and prompts share a bounded readout) the model drifts toward refusing everything, its mean training reward rising to zero like $1/t$ in training time $t$, so the curve reads as improvement while coverage collapses. The advantage estimator compounds the failure: in its sparse-answer regime, group normalization silently replaces every designed penalty with an effective penalty of one, moving the learned threshold from $t^\ast$ to $1/2$. The repair is structural: train a mandatory confidence report with a strictly proper score plus a correctness reward, and abstain only at deployment by thresholding the report. The always-emitted report has no gate to saturate, so no shared factor can kill its reward gradient and its anchor together, and its calibrated optimum is attracting. Simulations confirm every prediction, and experiments on language models at two scales confirm the mechanism live: the rule silences questions the models demonstrably still solve within ten optimizer steps, an ablation isolates the cause, and report-level training raises coverage, accuracy, and calibration together.

</details>


### [246] [Agentic Bayesian Optimization through Surrogate-Augmented Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00316)
*Paul Brunzema,Louis Tiao,Nhat Le,Kevin De Angeli,Yao Xuan,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"代理式贝叶斯优化"（agentic Bayesian optimization）范式，让LLM代理作为BO循环中的核心决策者，同时通过贝叶斯后端提供不确定性感知的优化基础。作者实现了Sara（代理增强型自动研究代理）和lenz（模块化BoTorch后端），实验表明该方法在合成和真实基准上保持了最优BO的可靠性，超越LLM基线，并能利用自然语言先验改进优化效果，还支持动态设置中的在线问题重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化依赖通用统计先验，虽然原理上更丰富的领域先验能提升性能，但通过定制核函数或问题结构编码先验非常困难且很少在实践中实现。现有的基于LLM的BO方法要么将LLM固定在特定角色中，要么赋予其过大的控制权而牺牲了BO的系统性探索能力，需要一个既能利用LLM非正式先验又保持BO可靠性的新范式。

Method: 提出代理式贝叶斯优化范式：LLM代理作为BO循环的中心决策者，负责配置问题、查询贝叶斯后端、选择和提交评估，并可在运行过程中修订优化策略（调整边界、切换采集函数、提出针对性评估或重新框定问题）。核心实现包括Sara（代理增强型自动研究代理）和lenz（模块化BoTorch后端），通过结构化接口让代理可以检查和修改后端。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中，Sara无需先验知识即保持最优BO的可靠性，超越LLM基线的性能；利用自然语言先验时能进一步超越标准BO。在动态设置中，Sara能够根据需求变化实时重构完整优化问题，展示了标准BO不具备的能力。

Conclusion: 代理式贝叶斯优化成功结合了LLM的领域知识利用能力和BO的系统性、可靠性优势，为将非正式先验（自然语言、代码、文档）纳入优化流程提供了一条实用路径，并解锁了动态问题重构这一全新能力。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) has become the standard tool for sample-efficient optimization and owes its efficiency to uncertainty-aware search driven by generic statistical priors. Richer domain priors can improve BO in principle, but encoding them through tailored kernels or problem structure is difficult and rarely done in practice. LLMs can help sidestep this difficulty by making informal priors from natural language, code, and documentation directly available to the optimizer. However, existing LLM-based BO methods either insert the LLM into a fixed role (surrogate, acquisition proxy, or configuration interface) or hand it broad control, sacrificing the systematic exploration that makes BO reliable. We introduce agentic Bayesian optimization: a paradigm in which an LLM agent is the central decision maker in the BO loop while a Bayesian backend provides the uncertainty-aware optimization substrate. The agent configures the problem, queries the backend, selects and commits evaluations, and can revise the optimization strategy during the run by tightening bounds, switching acquisition functions, proposing targeted evaluations, or even reframing the problem following new instructions or observed evidence. We instantiate this idea in Sara, a surrogate-augmented autoresearch agent, and lenz, a modular BoTorch-based backend that the agent can inspect and modify through a structured interface. Across synthetic and real-world benchmarks, Sara preserves the reliability of state-of-the-art BO without prior knowledge, outperforms LLM-based baselines, and uses natural-language priors to improve beyond standard BO. We further demonstrate the practical value of agentic BO in dynamic settings, where Sara reconfigures the full optimization problem on the fly as requirements change, a capability not previously available in standard BO.

</details>


### [247] [Neural operator learning for collision-aware trajectory planning of spacecraft swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00320)
*Sidhdharth D. Sikka,Suyi Gao,Zehui Lu,Rongjie Lai,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种置换等变神经算子，可在单次前向传播中为整个航天器集群生成碰撞感知轨迹规划，配备批量高斯-牛顿精修以保证精确轨道动力学，无需最优轨迹标签训练，可从10个航天器零样本泛化到1000个航天器及11000个碎片场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轨迹优化方法在航天器集群规模增大时，由于两两安全约束数量激增导致求解效率急剧下降；而基于学习的规划器难以在不同集群规模和碎片密度间泛化，无法满足日益拥挤轨道环境下对高效、可扩展轨迹规划的需求。

Method: 提出置换等变神经算子，将航天器、目标和碎片的分布映射为整个集群的碰撞感知轨迹（单次前向传播完成）；配合批量高斯-牛顿精修环节强制执行精确轨道动力学。训练采用自监督物理目标函数而非最优轨迹标签，同时结合针对自身推演轨迹生成的对抗威胁进行训练。

Result: 在10个航天器上训练后，可零样本泛化到包含11000余个编目对象的1000航天器集群，轨迹精度媲美单智能体最优控制求解器，能规避碎片盲基线无法应对的最坏情况威胁，并将集群内部邻近度降低数倍。

Conclusion: 基于物理的算子学习为拥挤轨道中的轨迹规划提供了快速、可扩展的替代方案，克服了传统最优控制的规模瓶颈，展现了从少量样本到大规模复杂场景的强大泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft swarms must plan fuel-efficient, collision-free maneuvers in increasingly congested orbits, yet classical trajectory optimization scales poorly as pairwise safety constraints multiply with swarm size, and learning-based planners rarely transfer across swarm sizes or debris densities. Here we introduce a permutation-equivariant neural operator that maps distributions of spacecraft, targets and debris to collision-aware trajectories for an entire swarm in a single forward pass, paired with a batched Gauss-Newton finish that enforces exact orbital dynamics. The operator is trained without optimal-trajectory labels, combining self-supervised physics objectives with adversarial threats generated against its own rollouts. Trained on ten spacecraft, it generalizes zero-shot to swarms of 1,000 amid more than 11,000 catalogued objects, matching a per-agent optimal-control solver's accuracy, evading worst-case threats that a debris-blind baseline cannot, and reducing proximity within the swarm several-fold. Physics-grounded operator learning thus offers a fast, scalable alternative to optimal control for crowded orbits.

</details>


### [248] [Ensemble of Unsupervised Deep Learning for Clustering Imbalanced Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00346)
*Pulock Das,Yina Hou,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究深度聚类方法在数据不平衡场景下的性能，提出两种集成方法（跨嵌入维度集成和多数投票集成），在16个二分类表格数据集上验证了集成方法在ACC、NMI和ARI指标上优于单一深度聚类方法，表明深度聚类可作为不平衡数据下监督分类的强有力替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督分类在数据不平衡时存在多数类偏差问题，导致假阴性和准确率高估。深度聚类因无需类标签进行表征学习，理论上可免受类别不平衡影响，但其在不平衡数据下的表现尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 提出两种聚类集成方法：(1)跨不同嵌入维度聚合深度聚类分配结果；(2)对表现最佳的聚类算法进行多数投票。实验在16个二分类表格数据集上人为诱导不同不平衡程度进行验证。

Result: 不同深度聚类方法在不同不平衡水平下展现出不同优势。两种集成方法在ACC、NMI和ARI三项指标上平均优于单一聚类方法，对数据不平衡具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在无监督条件下，深度聚类能够有效识别不平衡数据中的真实类别，可作为监督分类在数据不平衡场景下的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Data imbalance poses a major challenge in supervised classification, where the majority-class bias contributes to false negatives and overestimates classification accuracy. Unsupervised deep clustering can be immune to class imbalance because representation learning for clustering is performed without class labels. Deep clustering has been proposed for images, languages, and graphs, while its application to tabular data has only emerged recently. This paper is among the first to examine the performance of state-of-the-art deep clustering methods under varying levels of data imbalance. We introduce two novel cluster ensemble approaches: one aggregates deep clustering assignments across different embedding dimensions, and the other applies majority voting to the best-performing clustering algorithms. Experiments on 16 binary tabular datasets with varying and artificially induced levels of imbalance reveal distinct strengths of different deep clustering methods. On average, our ensemble methods outperform individual clustering methods in ACC, NMI, and ARI scores, offering greater resilience to data imbalance when identifying ground-truth classes without supervision. Therefore, in an imbalanced data scenario, deep clustering can serve as a strong alternative to supervised classification.

</details>


### [249] [Modeling Unknown Nonlocal PDE Systems via Flow Map Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00400)
*Zhongshu Xu,Ying Li,Yanzhi Zhang,Dongbin Xiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流图学习（FML）框架，用于直接从解数据中学习未知的非局部偏微分方程（PDE），无需近似或评估底层的非局部算子。该方法在模态空间或节点空间中学习有限时间演化算子，并通过一维和二维分数阶扩散方程及波动方程的数值实验验证了其准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部偏微分方程在许多应用中出现，但由于非局部算子的存在，其建模和学习通常非常困难。现有的方法往往需要近似底层非局部算子，这带来了额外的复杂性和误差。

Method: 提出流图学习（FML）框架，不学习或近似非局部算子本身，而是在模态空间或节点空间中学习有限时间演化算子。针对谱表示和基于网格的表示分别开发了两种互补的公式化方法。

Result: 在一维和二维分数阶扩散方程及波动方程上的数值实验表明，仅使用短观察窗口即可实现准确且稳定的长期预测。该方法为学习未知非局部动力学提供了有效的数据驱动框架，且无需显式评估非局部算子。

Conclusion: 所提出的FML框架能够有效规避非局部算子的显式评估难题，通过直接学习有限时间演化算子来实现非局部PDE的数据驱动建模，并在长时预测中表现出准确性和稳定性。

Abstract: Nonlocal partial differential equations arise in many applications but are often difficult to model and learn because of the presence of nonlocal operators. We present a flow-map learning (FML) framework for modeling unknown nonlocal PDEs directly from solution data. Rather than learning or approximating the underlying nonlocal operators, the proposed approach learns the finite-time evolution operator in either modal or nodal space. Two complementary formulations are developed for spectral and grid-based solution representations. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional fractional diffusion and wave equations demonstrate accurate and stable long-time prediction using only short observation windows. The proposed approach provides an effective data-driven framework for learning unknown nonlocal dynamics without explicit evaluation of nonlocal operators.

</details>


### [250] [DSETA: A Dual-Stage Continual Learning Framework for Travel Time Prediction in Dynamic Traffic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00402)
*Yanming Lyu,Yue Cheng,Lingkun Li,Ruipeng Gao,Xinyue Liu,Hui Gao,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSETA，一个双阶段增量更新的ETA预测框架，通过将连续学习分为日内和日间两个阶段，分别处理短期交通波动和长期分布偏移，并引入历史交通知识整合模块防止灾难性遗忘，在滴滴真实数据集上取得了显著的MAE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETA预测方法无法适应大城市中日益动态的交通拥堵模式，要么无法应对不规则的交通模式和突发拥堵，要么在处理新数据分布时未能将长期趋势与短期波动分离，导致模型在真实场景中性能下降。

Method: 提出DSETA框架，包含两个阶段的持续学习：(1)日内学习阶段，完全依赖实时数据动态适应假期或事故等引起的短期交通模式；(2)日间学习阶段，利用短时间窗口的聚合历史数据捕获长期分布偏移知识（如季节性趋势和交通网络演化）。此外设计了历史交通知识整合模块来防止灾难性遗忘，保留常规模式知识。

Result: 在北京、武汉和西安三个城市的在线A/B测试中，MAE分别降低了6.62%、0.73%和2.40%。该框架已成功部署在滴滴生产环境中，每天处理数亿次请求。

Conclusion: DSETA通过双阶段持续学习框架有效解决了ETA预测中短期波动与长期分布偏移的适应问题，在工业级大规模场景中展现了强劲的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Estimated Time of Arrival (ETA) prediction is a core component of intelligent transportation systems. As traffic congestion patterns become increasingly dynamic in large cities, maintaining high prediction accuracy poses a major challenge for ride-hailing platforms. Existing methods either fail to adapt to irregular traffic patterns and sudden congestion, or suffer from new distributions without disentangling long-term trends from short-term fluctuations, thereby degrading model performance in real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose DSETA, an incrementally updated Dual-Stage ETA prediction framework. Specifically, the continual learning process is divided into \textit{inter-day} and \textit{intra-day} stages. We first design the \textit{intra-day} learning stage, which relies entirely on real-time data to enable dynamic adaptation to short-term traffic patterns caused by events like holidays or accidents. Next, we develop the \textit{inter-day} learning stage, which leverages aggregated historical data from a short time window to capture knowledge of long-term distribution shifts, such as seasonal trends and traffic network evolution. Subsequently, to prevent catastrophic forgetting and preserve knowledge of regular patterns, we explore a \textit{Historical Traffic Knowledge Consolidation} module. Finally, we validate DSETA's effectiveness and robustness through extensive offline and online experiments conducted on real-world datasets from DiDi's platform.
